-m_{1}&-m_{2}&-m_{3}\end{pmatrix}=(-1)^{l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}}\begin{pmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
m_{1}&m_{2}&m_{3}\end{pmatrix}=(-1)^{l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}}\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right)_{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}},

(421)

since
m_{1}+m_{2}+m_{3}=0
. Even permutations of the
3j
-symbols have the
same value,

\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right)_{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}=\left(l_{2}\,l_{3}\,l_{1}\right)_{m_{2}m_{3}m_{1}}=\left(l_{3}\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right)_{m_{3}m_{1}m_{2}},

(422)

while odd permutations are given by

\left(l_{2}\,l_{1}\,l_{3}\right)_{m_{2}m_{1}m_{3}}=\left(l_{1}\,l_{3}\,l_{2}\right)_{m_{1}m_{3}m_{2}}=\left(l_{3}\,l_{2}\,l_{1}\right)_{m_{3}m_{2}m_{1}}=(-1)^{l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}}\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right)_{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}.

(423)

The above equalities hold even when some of the azimuthal indices are
raised on both sides of the equations.

The orthogonality relations of the
3j
-symbol are represented by

\displaystyle\left(l\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right)_{mm_{1}m_{2}}\left(l^{\prime}\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right)_{m^{\prime}}^{\phantom{m^{\prime}}m_{1}m_{2}}=\frac{(-1)^{l+l_{1}+l_{2}}\delta_{ll^{\prime}}}{2l+1}g^{(l)}_{mm^{\prime}}\delta^{\triangle}_{l\,l_{1}l_{2}},

(424)

\displaystyle\sum_{l}(-1)^{l}(2l+1)\left(l\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right)_{mm_{1}m_{2}}\left(l\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right)^{m}_{\phantom{m}m_{1}^{\prime}m_{2}^{\prime}}=(-1)^{l_{1}+l_{2}}g^{(l_{1})}_{m_{1}m_{1}^{\prime}}g^{(l_{2})}_{m_{2}m_{2}^{\prime}},

(425)

where
\delta^{\triangle}_{l_{1}l_{2}l_{3}}=1
only when
(l_{1},l_{2},l_{3})

satisfies the triangle inequality, and is zero otherwise. From
Eq. (
424
), we have a derived formula,

\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right)_{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right)^{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}=(-1)^{l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}}\delta^{\triangle}_{l_{1}l_{2}l_{3}}.

(426)

When one or two of the
l
’s in the
3j
-symbols are zero, we have

\left(l\,0\,0\right)_{m00}=\delta_{l0}\delta_{m0},\qquad\left(l\,l^{\prime}\,0\right)_{mm^{\prime}0}=\frac{(-1)^{l}\delta_{ll^{\prime}}}{\sqrt{2l+1}}g^{(l)}_{mm^{\prime}}.

(427)

In this paper, the Gaunt integral with our notation is denoted by

\left[l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right]_{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}\equiv\int\frac{d^{2}n}{4\pi}\,C_{l_{1}m_{1}}(\hat{\bm{n}})C_{l_{2}m_{2}}(\hat{\bm{n}})C_{l_{3}m_{3}}(\hat{\bm{n}})=\begin{pmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
0&0&0\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
m_{1}&m_{2}&m_{3}\end{pmatrix},

(428)

and azimuthal indices are raised and lowered by the spherical metric

g_{(l)}^{mm^{\prime}}
,
g^{(l)}_{mm^{\prime}}
as in the case of
3j
-symbol.
A
3j
-symbol with vanishing
m
’s are nonzero only when the sum of

l
’s are even, i.e.,

\begin{pmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
0&0&0\end{pmatrix}=0,\qquad\mathrm{when}\ \ l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}=\mathrm{odd}.

(429)

Correspondingly, the Gaunt integrals are nonzero only when

l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}=\mathrm{even}
in the above equation. The covariant
version of the Gaunt integral has the same components as the
contravariant one:

\left[l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right]_{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}=\left[l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right]^{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}},

(430)

because of a symmetry of the
3j
-symbols, Eq. (
421
).
The Gaunt integrals are totally symmetric under the permutation,

\left[l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right]_{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}=\left[l_{2}\,l_{3}\,l_{1}\right]_{m_{2}m_{3}m_{1}}=\left[l_{3}\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right]_{m_{3}m_{1}m_{2}}=\left[l_{2}\,l_{1}\,l_{3}\right]_{m_{2}m_{1}m_{3}}=\left[l_{1}\,l_{3}\,l_{2}\right]_{m_{1}m_{3}m_{2}}=\left[l_{3}\,l_{2}\,l_{1}\right]_{m_{3}m_{2}m_{1}}.

(431)

An orthogonality relation of the
3j
-symbols, Eq. (
424
),
indicates the orthogonality relation of the Gaunt integral:

\left[l\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right]_{mm_{1}m_{2}}\left[l^{\prime}\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right]_{m^{\prime}}^{\phantom{m^{\prime}}m_{1}m_{2}}=\frac{1}{2l+1}\begin{pmatrix}l&l_{1}&l_{2}\\
0&0&0\end{pmatrix}^{2}\delta_{ll^{\prime}}g^{(l)}_{mm^{\prime}}.

(432)

When one or two of the
l
’s in the Gaunt integral are zero, we have

\left[l\,0\,0\right]_{m00}=\delta_{l0}\delta_{m0},\qquad\left[l\,l^{\prime}\,0\right]_{mm^{\prime}0}=\frac{\delta_{ll^{\prime}}}{2l+1}g^{(l)}_{mm^{\prime}}.

(433)

The Wigner’s
6j
-symbol is associated with the recoupling of three
angular momenta, and defined by

\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\end{Bmatrix}=(-1)^{l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}+l_{4}+l_{5}+l_{6}}\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right)^{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}\left(l_{1}\,l_{5}\,l_{6}\right)_{m_{1}}^{\phantom{m_{1}}m_{5}m_{6}}\left(l_{4}\,l_{2}\,l_{6}\right)^{m_{4}}_{\phantom{m_{4}}m_{2}m_{6}}\left(l_{4}\,l_{5}\,l_{3}\right)_{m_{4}m_{5}m_{3}}.

(434)

or equivalently,

\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\end{Bmatrix}\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right)_{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}=(-1)^{l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}}\left(l_{1}\,l_{5}\,l_{6}\right)_{m_{1}m_{5}m_{6}}\left(l_{4}\,l_{2}\,l_{6}\right)_{m_{4}m_{2}}^{\phantom{m_{4}m_{2}}m_{6}}\left(l_{4}\,l_{5}\,l_{3}\right)^{m_{4}m_{5}}_{\phantom{m_{4}m_{5}}m_{3}}.

(435)

The
6j
-symbols are invariant against any permutation of the columns:

\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{2}&l_{3}&l_{1}\\
l_{5}&l_{6}&l_{4}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{3}&l_{1}&l_{2}\\
l_{6}&l_{4}&l_{5}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{2}&l_{1}&l_{3}\\
l_{5}&l_{4}&l_{6}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{3}&l_{2}\\
l_{4}&l_{6}&l_{5}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{3}&l_{2}&l_{1}\\
l_{6}&l_{5}&l_{4}\end{Bmatrix},

(436)

and also invariant against the interchange of the upper and lower
arguments in each of any two columns,

\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{5}&l_{6}\\
l_{4}&l_{2}&l_{3}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{4}&l_{2}&l_{6}\\
l_{1}&l_{5}&l_{3}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{3}\\
l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{6}\end{Bmatrix}.

(437)

The Wigner’s
9j
-symbol is associated with the recoupling of four
angular momenta, and defined by

\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\\
l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\end{Bmatrix}=\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,l_{3}\right)^{m_{1}m_{2}m_{3}}\left(l_{4}\,l_{5}\,l_{6}\right)^{m_{4}m_{5}m_{6}}\left(l_{7}\,l_{8}\,l_{9}\right)^{m_{7}m_{8}m_{9}}\left(l_{1}\,l_{4}\,l_{7}\right)_{m_{1}m_{4}m_{7}}\left(l_{2}\,l_{5}\,l_{8}\right)_{m_{2}m_{5}m_{8}}\left(l_{3}\,l_{6}\,l_{9}\right)_{m_{3}m_{6}m_{9}}.

(438)

The
9j
-symbols are invariant under transposition,

\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\\
l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{4}&l_{7}\\
l_{2}&l_{5}&l_{8}\\
l_{3}&l_{6}&l_{9}\end{Bmatrix}.

(439)

and also invariant against cyclic (even) permutations of the columns
or lows:

\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\\
l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{2}&l_{3}&l_{1}\\
l_{5}&l_{6}&l_{4}\\
l_{8}&l_{9}&l_{7}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{3}&l_{1}&l_{2}\\
l_{6}&l_{4}&l_{5}\\
l_{9}&l_{7}&l_{8}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\\
l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\\
l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\\
l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\end{Bmatrix}.

(440)

They change the sign by noncyclic (odd) permutations of the columns
or lows, if the sum of all the arguments is odd:

(-1)^{R}\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\\
l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{2}&l_{1}&l_{3}\\
l_{5}&l_{4}&l_{6}\\
l_{8}&l_{7}&l_{9}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{3}&l_{2}&l_{1}\\
l_{6}&l_{5}&l_{4}\\
l_{9}&l_{8}&l_{7}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{3}&l_{2}\\
l_{4}&l_{6}&l_{5}\\
l_{7}&l_{9}&l_{8}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\\
l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\\
l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\end{Bmatrix}=\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&l_{3}\\
l_{7}&l_{8}&l_{9}\\
l_{4}&l_{5}&l_{6}\end{Bmatrix},

(441)

where

R=l_{1}+l_{2}+l_{3}+l_{4}+l_{5}+l_{6}+l_{7}+l_{8}+l_{9}.

(442)

Many formulas among
3j
-,
6j
-, and
9j
-symbols, including
orthogonality relations and sum rules, can be found in the standard
literature mentioned above. In particular, we use the following
sum rules in this paper for products of
3j
-symbols:

\left(l_{1}\,l_{2}\,L\right)_{m_{1}m_{2}}^{\phantom{m_{1}m_{2}}M}\left(L\,l_{3}\,l_{4}\right)_{Mm_{3}m_{4}}=(-1)^{l_{2}+l_{3}}\sum_{L^{\prime}}(2L^{\prime}+1)\left(l_{1}\,l_{3}\,L^{\prime}\right)_{m_{1}m_{3}}^{\phantom{m_{1}m_{3}}M^{\prime}}\left(L^{\prime}\,l_{2}\,l_{4}\right)_{M^{\prime}m_{2}m_{4}}\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{2}&L\\
l_{4}&l_{3}&L^{\prime}\end{Bmatrix},

(443)

\left(L\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right)_{M}^{\phantom{M}m_{1}m_{2}}\left(L^{\prime}\,l_{1}^{\prime}\,l_{2}^{\prime}\right)_{M^{\prime}}^{\phantom{M^{\prime}}m_{1}^{\prime}m_{2}^{\prime}}\left(l_{2}^{\prime}\,l_{2}\,l_{2}^{\prime\prime}\right)_{m_{2}^{\prime}m_{2}}^{\phantom{m_{2}^{\prime}m_{2}}m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}=\sum_{l_{1}^{\prime\prime},L^{\prime\prime}}(2l_{1}^{\prime\prime}+1)(2L^{\prime\prime}+1)\left(L\,L^{\prime}\,L^{\prime\prime}\right)_{MM^{\prime}}^{\phantom{MM^{\prime}}M^{\prime\prime}}\left(L^{\prime\prime}\,l_{2}^{\prime\prime}\,l_{1}^{\prime\prime}\right)_{M^{\prime\prime}}^{\phantom{m^{\prime\prime}}m_{2}^{\prime\prime}m_{1}^{\prime\prime}}\left(l_{1}\,l_{1}^{\prime}\,l_{1}^{\prime\prime}\right)^{m_{1}m_{1}^{\prime}}_{\phantom{m_{1}m_{1}^{\prime}}m_{1}^{\prime\prime}}\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{1}^{\prime}&l_{1}^{\prime\prime}\\
l_{2}&l_{2}^{\prime}&l_{2}^{\prime\prime}\\
L&L^{\prime}&L^{\prime\prime}\end{Bmatrix},

(444)

and

\left(L\,l_{1}\,l_{2}\right)_{M}^{\phantom{M}m_{1}m_{2}}\left(L^{\prime}\,l_{1}^{\prime}\,l_{2}^{\prime}\right)_{M^{\prime}}^{\phantom{M^{\prime}}m_{1}^{\prime}m_{2}^{\prime}}\left(l_{1}^{\prime}\,l_{1}\,l_{1}^{\prime\prime}\right)_{m_{1}^{\prime}m_{1}}^{\phantom{m_{1}^{\prime}m_{1}}m_{1}^{\prime\prime}}\left(l_{2}^{\prime}\,l_{2}\,l_{2}^{\prime\prime}\right)_{m_{2}^{\prime}m_{2}}^{\phantom{m_{2}^{\prime}m_{2}}m_{2}^{\prime\prime}}=\sum_{L^{\prime\prime}}(2L^{\prime\prime}+1)\left(L\,L^{\prime}\,L^{\prime\prime}\right)_{MM^{\prime}}^{\phantom{MM^{\prime}}M^{\prime\prime}}\left(L^{\prime\prime}\,l_{2}^{\prime\prime}\,l_{1}^{\prime\prime}\right)_{M^{\prime\prime}}^{\phantom{m^{\prime\prime}}m_{2}^{\prime\prime}m_{1}^{\prime\prime}}\begin{Bmatrix}l_{1}&l_{1}^{\prime}&l_{1}^{\prime\prime}\\
l_{2}&l_{2}^{\prime}&l_{2}^{\prime\prime}\\
L&L^{\prime}&L^{\prime\prime}\end{Bmatrix}.

(445)
